[Icemule Trace, Town Center] (4042150)
A bustling square dusted with snow sprawls beneath a sturdy mule statue standing placidly atop a tall plinth of silver-veined black stone.  The sun filters through the ice of the statue, showering the throng with a kaleidoscope of rainbowed shards.  Some denizens, mostly halflings but with a scattering of other folk mixed in, chatter by a roaring bonfire, lazing on a smooth dark stone bench or reclining on colorful cloaks spread out on the ground.  Others mill around a burled pine message board standing close to a colorful silver-bound barrel.  You also see a jet black caracal, a stalk of bluebells, a stalk of bluebells, the Barjnibus disk, the Ankerath disk, the discordant Siarnaq disk, a flying brown leather tome, a blue-eyed charcoal cat, the earth brown Keimungarr disk, a grey wolf that is sitting, a warm double chocolate brownie with wine-infused cherries, a large hairy spider, a piece of cherry cordial chocolate, a branch-carved wooden bowl of hot dark chocolate, a white chocolate empath, the Cranbesh disk and a woven bark wastebin.
Also here: Traider, Livianna, Jzhensen, Barjnibus, Ankerath, Siarnaq, Gondavyn, Lord Keimungarr, Legendary Noble Ethrelas, Noble Cranbesh
Obvious paths: north, northeast, east, south, west

Some denizens, mostly halflings but with a scattering of other folk mixed in, chatter by a roaring bonfire, lazing on a smooth dark stone bench or reclining on colorful cloaks spread out on the ground.  Others mill around a burled pine message board standing close to a colorful silver-bound barrel.  You also see the Maliku disk, the Valandryn disk, a joyous forest spirit that is flying around, the gold-dusted Moomfs disk crowned with sylvarraend rubies, an impish snow spirit that is flying around, a jet black caracal, a stalk of bluebells, a stalk of bluebells, the Ankerath disk, the discordant Siarnaq disk, a flying brown leather tome, a blue-eyed charcoal cat, the earth brown Keimungarr disk, a grey wolf that is sitting, a warm double chocolate brownie with wine-infused cherries, a large hairy spider, a piece of cherry cordial chocolate, a branch-carved wooden bowl of hot dark chocolate, a white chocolate empath, the Cranbesh disk and a woven bark wastebin.
Also here: Maliku, Zhelas, Lord Valandryn, Instructor Moomfs who is sitting, Jzhensen, Ankerath, Siarnaq, Gondavyn, Lord Keimungarr, Legendary Noble Ethrelas, Noble Cranbesh

[Icemule Trace, South Road - 2302] (u4042229)
The silvery sheen of a statue to the north glints merrily over the heads of most passersby.  Halflings and sylvankind in the deep blue elk hide livery of the Silvermule Gaming Hall rush past, messenger bags thumping against their sides as they race off to deliver letters and missives.  Most of the houses and businesses to either side of the street are built from grey stone, but their doors are brightly painted with a hodgepodge of colors.  An incongruous stone doorframe stands off to the side of the street.  You also see a Silvermule courier.

[Icemule Trace, South Road - 2409] (u4042228)

The savory aroma of baked apples tantalizes from an open window at street level.  Less pleasant, though mostly muted by hanging nosegays of rosebay and bilberry, is the odor of dyes wafting from a colorful canvas pavilion on the opposite side of the road.

[Icemule Trace, Town Center - 2300] (u4042150)
A bustling square dusted with snow sprawls beneath a sturdy mule statue standing placidly atop a tall plinth of silver-veined black stone.  The sun filters through the ice of the statue, showering the throng with a kaleidoscope of rainbowed shards.  Some denizens, mostly halflings but with a scattering of other folk mixed in, chatter by a roaring bonfire, lazing on a smooth dark stone bench or reclining on colorful cloaks spread out on the ground.  Others mill around a burled pine message board standing close to a colorful silver-bound barrel. Two-story rowhouses painted in vivid colors line Porter Street.  On the steps of a blue and white house, an older halfling woman sits, peeling apples and watching the people passing by.  Opposite the homes, a colorful wooden sign hangs next to the door of a dyer's shop.  Several of the homes burn herbs in their windows to keep out the faint stench of the dyes.

[Tavern Burrow, Ale Street - 2400] (u4042214)
A stucco well has attracted a small group of children, who drop stones into its depths and hoot with glee at each muffled splash.  Across the cobblestone street, an enterprising group of young toughs has erected a hoarbeam trestle table.  They work industriously at picking the chests and coffers of local adventurers, one lad sitting atop an old oak beer barrel.  Lighter cobbles on the road form the outline of a frothing tankard, and the nearest lamp posts have bases wrought in the shapes of dancing revelers.  You also see a wry halfling trickster.

[Icemule Trace, Town Center - 2300] (u4042150)
A bustling square dusted with snow sprawls beneath a sturdy mule statue standing placidly atop a tall plinth of silver-veined black stone.  The sun filters through the ice of the statue, showering the throng with a kaleidoscope of rainbowed shards.  Some denizens, mostly halflings but with a scattering of other folk mixed in, chatter by a roaring bonfire, lazing on a smooth dark stone bench or reclining on colorful cloaks spread out on the ground.  Others mill around a burled pine message board standing close to a colorful silver-bound barrel.  You also see the discordant Siarnaq disk, a blue-eyed charcoal cat, a flying brown leather tome, the icy blue Elvidor disk, the Ankerath disk, a jet black caracal, the sparkling Endaru disk, the Maliku disk, the Tauxyn disk, a stalk of bluebells, a stalk of bluebells, a warm double chocolate brownie with wine-infused cherries, a large hairy spider, a piece of cherry cordial chocolate, a branch-carved wooden bowl of hot dark chocolate, a white chocolate empath, the Cranbesh disk and a woven bark wastebin.
